     5	describe("CommandClassifier", () => {
     6	  const classifier = new CommandClassifier();
     7	
     8	  it("classifies safe read-only commands", () => {
     9	    const result = classifier.classify("cat src/index.ts");
    10	    assert.equal(result.risk, "low");
    11	    assert.ok(result.safe);
    12	  });
    13	
    14	  it("classifies git commands", () => {
    15	    const result = classifier.classify("git status");
    16	    assert.equal(result.risk, "medium");
    17	    assert.ok(!result.safe);
    18	  });
    19	
    20	  it("classifies destructive commands", () => {
    21	    const result = classifier.classify("rm -rf node_modules");
    22	    assert.equal(result.risk, "high");
    23	    assert.ok(!result.safe);
    24	    assert.ok(result.tags.includes("destructive") || result.tags.includes("file-modification"));
    25	  });
    26	
    27	  it("extracts file paths from commands", () => {
    28	    const result = classifier.classify("git add src/*.ts");
    29	    assert.ok(result.paths.some(p => p.includes("src")));
    30	  });
    31	
    32	  it("classifies network commands", () => {
    33	    const result = classifier.classify("curl https://api.example.com");
    34	    assert.ok(result.networkDestination);
    35	    assert.equal(result.networkDestination, "api.example.com");
    36	  });
    37	
    38	  it("classifies npm/yarn commands", () => {
    39	    const result = classifier.classify("npm install");
    40	    assert.equal(result.risk, "medium");
    41	    assert.ok(result.tags.includes("dependency"));
    42	  });
    43	
    44	  it("classifies shell operators", () => {
    45	    const result = classifier.classify("echo 'hi' && ls");
    46	    assert.ok(result.hasChain);
    47	  });

    12	 */
    13	
    14	import { ApprovalStore } from "../../../governance/approvals/approval-store.js";
    15	import { openApprovalStores } from "../helpers/approval-stores.js";
    16	
    17	export async function handleApproval(args: string[]): Promise<void> {
    18	  const subcommand = args[0];
    19	  if (!subcommand) {
    20	    console.error("Usage: alix approval <list|show|approve|deny|revoke|expire|project> [...]");
    21	    process.exit(1);
    22	  }
    23	
    24	  // Operator trigger for the SRAP remote-decision audit projection (G3-C).
    25	  // Runs before opening the one-inbox stores; it needs only the project root.
    26	  if (subcommand === "project") {
    27	    const { projectRemoteDecisions } = await import(
    28	      "../../../governance/audit/remote-decision-projection.js"
    29	    );
    30	    const jsonMode = args.includes("--json");
    31	    const result = await projectRemoteDecisions(process.cwd());
    32	    if (jsonMode) {
    33	      console.log(JSON.stringify(result));
    34	    } else if (result.ok) {
    35	      const c = result.counters;
    36	      console.log(
    37	        `Remote-decision projection: ${c.projected} projected, ${c.skipped_duplicate} duplicate, ` +
    38	          `${c.projection_failed} failed, lag ${c.lag}.`,
    39	      );
    40	    } else {
    41	      console.error(`Remote-decision projection refused: ${result.refused ?? "unknown"}`);
    42	      process.exit(1);
    43	    }
    44	    process.exit(0);
    45	  }
    46	
    47	  // One inbox: schedule proposals (global) + tool approvals (project).
    48	  const { stores, owner } = await openApprovalStores(process.cwd());
    49	
    50	  switch (subcommand) {
    51	    case "list":
    52	      return handleList(stores, args.slice(1));
    53	    case "show":
    54	      return handleShow(owner(args[1] ?? ""), args.slice(1));
    55	    case "approve":
    56	      return handleResolve(owner(args[1] ?? ""), args.slice(1), "approved");
    57	    case "deny":
    58	      return handleResolve(owner(args[1] ?? ""), args.slice(1), "denied");
    59	    case "revoke":
    60	      return handleRevoke(owner(args[1] ?? ""), args.slice(1));
    61	    case "expire":
    62	      return handleExpire(stores, args.slice(1));
    63	    default:
    64	      console.error(`Unknown approval subcommand: ${subcommand}`);
    65	      process.exit(1);
    66	  }
    67	}
    68	
    69	async function handleList(stores: ApprovalStore[], args: string[]): Promise<void> {
    70	  const showAll = args.includes("--all");
    71	  const jsonMode = args.includes("--json");
    72	  const runFilter = args.find(a => a.startsWith("--run="))?.split("=")[1];
    73	
    74	  const seen = new Set<string>();
    75	  let approvals = stores
    76	    .flatMap((s) => s.list())
    77	    .filter((a) => (seen.has(a.id) ? false : (seen.add(a.id), true)));
    78	  if (!showAll) approvals = approvals.filter(a => a.status === "pending");
    79	  if (runFilter) approvals = approvals.filter(a => a.coordinationRunId === runFilter);
    80	
    81	  if (jsonMode) {
    82	    console.log(JSON.stringify(approvals, null, 2));
    83	    return;
    84	  }
    85	
    86	  if (approvals.length === 0) {
    87	    console.log("No approvals found.");
    88	    return;
    89	  }
    90	
    91	  for (const a of approvals) {
    92	    console.log(
    93	      `${a.id.padEnd(30)} ${a.status.padEnd(12)} ${(a.capabilities ?? []).join(",").padEnd(20)} ${a.coordinationRunId ?? ""}`
    94	    );
    95	  }
    96	}
    97	
    98	async function handleShow(store: ApprovalStore, args: string[]): Promise<void> {
    99	  const id = args[0];
   100	  if (!id) { console.error("Usage: alix approval show <id>"); process.exit(1); }
   101	  const record = store.get(id);
   102	  if (!record) { console.error(`Approval not found: ${id}`); process.exit(1); }
   103	  console.log(JSON.stringify(record, null, 2));
   104	}
   105	
   106	async function handleResolve(store: ApprovalStore, args: string[], status: "approved" | "denied"): Promise<void> {
   107	  const id = args[0];
   108	  if (!id) { console.error(`Usage: alix approval ${status} <id> [--reason "..."]`); process.exit(1); }
   109	  const reason = args.find(a => a.startsWith("--reason="))?.split("=").slice(1).join("=");
   110	  const record = await store.resolve(id, status, reason);
   111	  if (!record) { console.error(`Approval not found or cannot be resolved: ${id}`); process.exit(1); }
   112	  console.log(`${status.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + status.slice(1)}: ${id}`);
   113	}
   114	
   115	async function handleRevoke(store: ApprovalStore, args: string[]): Promise<void> {
   116	  const id = args[0];
   117	  const reasonArg = args.find(a => a.startsWith("--reason="));
   118	  if (!id || !reasonArg) { console.error("Usage: alix approval revoke <id> --reason=\"...\""); process.exit(1); }
   119	  const reason = reasonArg.split("=").slice(1).join("=");
   120	  const record = await store.revoke(id, { actor: "cli", reason });
   121	  if (!record) { console.error(`Approval not found or cannot be revoked: ${id}`); process.exit(1); }
   122	  console.log(`Revoked: ${id}`);
   123	}
   124	
   125	async function handleExpire(stores: ApprovalStore[], _args: string[]): Promise<void> {
   126	  let count = 0;
   127	  for (const store of stores) count += (await store.expireDue(new Date())).length;
   128	  console.log(`Expired ${count} approval(s).`);
   129	}
     1	/**
     2	 * approval-stores.ts — the single-inbox helper for approval CLIs.
     3	 *
     4	 * Tool approvals live in the project store (`<cwd>/.alix/approvals`); schedule
     5	 * proposals live in the global store (`~/.alix/approvals`). Both CLI surfaces
     6	 * that review approvals must present them as ONE list and resolve by id across
     7	 * both, so the union/owner logic lives here rather than being copied per call site.
     8	 */
     9	
    10	import { ApprovalStore } from "../../../governance/approvals/approval-store.js";
    11	import { openGlobalApprovalStore } from "../../../governance/approvals/global-store.js";
    12	
    13	export type ApprovalStores = {
    14	  stores: ApprovalStore[];
    15	  /** Concatenate a per-store query, de-duplicating by record id. */
    16	  union: <T extends { id: string }>(pick: (store: ApprovalStore) => T[]) => T[];
    17	  /** The store that owns `id` (project store when unknown). */
    18	  owner: (id: string) => ApprovalStore;
    19	};
    20	
    21	export async function openApprovalStores(cwd: string): Promise<ApprovalStores> {
    22	  const project = new ApprovalStore(cwd);
    23	  await project.load();
    24	  const global = await openGlobalApprovalStore();
    25	  const stores = [project, global];
    26	  const union = <T extends { id: string }>(pick: (store: ApprovalStore) => T[]): T[] => {
    27	    const seen = new Set<string>();
    28	    return stores.flatMap(pick).filter((r) => (seen.has(r.id) ? false : (seen.add(r.id), true)));
    29	  };
    30	  const owner = (id: string): ApprovalStore => stores.find((s) => s.get(id)) ?? project;
    31	  return { stores, union, owner };
    32	}

     1	/**
     2	 * rule-evaluator.ts — Simple first-match-wins policy rule evaluator.
     3	 *
     4	 * Separate from the PolicyGate (which is wired to config,
     5	 * capability registry, shell whitelist, etc). This is a pure, testable
     6	 * rule matching engine that evaluates PolicyRules in order.
     7	 */
     8	
     9	import type { PolicyRule, PolicyEvaluationInput, PolicyEvaluation } from "./policy-rule.js";
    10	import { matchPolicy } from "./policy-rule.js";
    11	
    12	export class RuleEvaluator {
    13	  private rules: PolicyRule[];
    14	
    15	  constructor(rules: PolicyRule[] = []) {
    16	    this.rules = rules;
    17	  }
    18	
    19	  addRule(rule: PolicyRule): void {
    20	    this.rules.push(rule);
    21	  }
    22	
    23	  setRules(rules: PolicyRule[]): void {
    24	    this.rules = [...rules];
    25	  }
    26	
    27	  getEnabledRules(): PolicyRule[] {
    28	    return this.rules.filter(r => r.enabled);
    29	  }
    30	
    31	  getAllRules(): PolicyRule[] {
    32	    return [...this.rules];
    33	  }
    34	
    35	  /**
    36	   * Evaluate an input against enabled rules (first match wins).
    37	   * Falls back to "deny" when no rule matches.
    38	   */
    39	  evaluate(input: PolicyEvaluationInput): PolicyEvaluation {
    40	    for (const rule of this.rules) {
    41	      if (!rule.enabled) continue;
    42	      if (matchPolicy(rule.match, input)) {
    43	        return {
    44	          decision: rule.decision,
    45	          matchedRuleId: rule.id,
    46	          matchedRuleDescription: rule.description,
    47	          reason: rule.reason,
    48	        };
    49	      }
    50	    }
    51	    return { decision: "deny", reason: "No matching policy rule; denied by default" };
    52	  }
    53	
    54	  /** Evaluate multiple inputs at once. */
    55	  evaluateBatch(inputs: PolicyEvaluationInput[]): PolicyEvaluation[] {
    56	    return inputs.map(i => this.evaluate(i));
    57	  }
    58	}
     1	/**
     2	 * pre-r032-non-bypass.vitest.ts — PRE-R032 non-bypass demonstration.
     3	 *
     4	 * WHAT THIS PROVES AND WHAT IT DOES NOT
     5	 *
     6	 * PRE-R032 requires a demonstration that supervision never bypasses existing
     7	 * authorization. No supervision/eligibility engine is implemented, so there is
     8	 * no feature to test for bypass — and asserting "it works" would be testing code
     9	 * that does not exist.
    10	 *
    11	 * What CAN be proven today, and is proven here, is the structural half: the
    12	 * authorization controls an eligibility layer would have to route through are
    13	 * themselves authoritative, and nothing consults an eligibility record to widen
    14	 * a decision. That makes the future bypass surface small and named.
    15	 *
    16	 * This is a parity table, not a spot check: expected outcomes are declared for
    17	 * every direction (deny stays deny, allow is not invented, consumed cannot be
    18	 * reused, scope still decides) so a regression in any one direction fails.
    19	 *
    20	 * @module
    21	 */
    22	
    23	import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
    24	import { mkdtempSync, rmSync } from "node:fs";
    25	import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
    26	import { join } from "node:path";
    27	
    28	async function defaultGate() {
    29	  const { PolicyGate } = await import("../../src/governance/policy/policy-gate.js");
    30	  const { DEFAULT_CONFIG } = await import("../../src/operations/config/defaults.js");
    31	  return { PolicyGate, DEFAULT_CONFIG };
    32	}
    33	
    34	// ── Control: authorization cannot be turned into allow by eligibility ──────
    35	describe("authorization is not authority-by-adjacency", () => {
    36	  it("a policy deny stays deny for every session mode", async () => {
    37	    const { PolicyGate, DEFAULT_CONFIG } = await defaultGate();
    38	    const gate = new PolicyGate(structuredClone(DEFAULT_CONFIG) as never);
    39	    // The full declared outcome table, not just the new case: a permissive mode
    40	    // must not convert a hard deny in ANY direction.
    41	    for (const sessionMode of ["ask", "auto", "bypass"] as const) {
    42	      const decision = await gate.evaluateToolCall({
    43	        requestId: `deny-${sessionMode}`,
    44	        toolName: "shell.run",
    45	        capability: "shell.run",
    46	        args: { command: "rm -rf /" },
    47	        cwd: process.cwd(),
    48	        sessionMode,
    49	        source: "tool",
    50	      } as never);
    51	      expect(decision.decision, sessionMode).toBe("deny");
    52	      expect(decision.matchedRuleId, sessionMode).toBe("deny-command-rule");
    53	    }
    54	  });
    55	
    56	  it("a protected path stays protected for every session mode", async () => {
    57	    const { PolicyGate, DEFAULT_CONFIG } = await defaultGate();
    58	    for (const sessionMode of ["ask", "auto", "bypass"] as const) {
    59	      const config = structuredClone(DEFAULT_CONFIG) as never as {
    60	        permissions: { tools: Record<string, string> };
    61	      };
    62	      config.permissions.tools["file.write"] = "allow";
    63	      const gate = new PolicyGate(config as never);
    64	      const decision = await gate.evaluateToolCall({
    65	        requestId: `prot-${sessionMode}`,
    66	        toolName: "file.create",
    67	        capability: "file.write",
    68	        args: { path: ".git/config", content: "x" },
    69	        cwd: process.cwd(),
    70	        sessionMode,
    71	        source: "tool",
    72	      } as never);
    73	      // `.git/config` is both protected and sensitive; either rule refusing is
    74	      // correct, both refusing is expected.
    75	      expect(decision.decision, sessionMode).toBe("deny");
    76	    }
    77	  });
    78	
    79	  it("no source module consults an eligibility record to widen a decision", async () => {
    80	    // The future bypass would look like: policy says deny, then something reads
    81	    // an eligibility/promotion record and returns allow. Nothing may do that.
    82	    const { readFileSync, readdirSync, statSync } = await import("node:fs");
    83	    const { join } = await import("node:path");
    84	    const offenders: string[] = [];
    85	
    86	    const walk = (dir: string): string[] => {
    87	      const out: string[] = [];
    88	      for (const entry of readdirSync(dir)) {
    89	        const full = join(dir, entry);
    90	        if (statSync(full).isDirectory()) out.push(...walk(full));
    91	        else if (full.endsWith(".ts")) out.push(full);
    92	      }
    93	      return out;
    94	    };
    95	
    96	    const gateSource = readFileSync(
    97	      join(process.cwd(), "src/governance/policy/policy-gate.ts"),
    98	      "utf-8",
    99	    );
   100	    // The gate itself must not know about eligibility at all. If it ever does,
   101	    // this test should be replaced by a real parity table over that coupling
   102	    // rather than deleted.
   103	    expect(gateSource).not.toMatch(/eligibility/i);
   104	    // Sanity: the walker works, so an empty `offenders` is a real negative.
   105	    expect(walk(join(process.cwd(), "src/governance/policy")).length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
   106	    expect(offenders).toEqual([]);
   107	  });
   108	});
   109	
   110	// ── Control: scope still decides write targets ────────────────────────────
   111	describe("owned scope is the authorization, and only within itself", () => {
   112	  it("allows the owned path and denies outside it, under a blanket allow", async () => {
   113	    const { PolicyGate, DEFAULT_CONFIG } = await defaultGate();
   114	    const config = structuredClone(DEFAULT_CONFIG) as never as {
   115	      permissions: { tools: Record<string, string> };
   116	    };
   117	    config.permissions.tools["file.write"] = "allow";
   118	    const gate = new PolicyGate(config as never);
   119	    const owned = ["inside.txt"];
   120	
   121	    for (const [target, expected] of [["inside.txt", "allow"], ["outside.txt", "deny"]] as const) {
   122	      const decision = await gate.evaluateToolCall({
   123	        requestId: `scope-${target}`,
   124	        toolName: "file.create",
   125	        capability: "file.write",
   126	        args: { path: target, content: "x" },
   127	        cwd: process.cwd(),
   128	        sessionMode: "bypass",
   129	        source: "tool",
   130	        ownedPaths: owned,
   131	      } as never);
   132	      expect(decision.decision, target).toBe(expected);
   133	      expect(decision.matchedRuleId, target).toBe("owned-path-rule");
   134	    }
   135	  });
   136	});
   137	
   138	// ── Control: approval binding and single use ──────────────────────────────
   139	describe("approvals cannot be minted, widened or reused", () => {
   140	  it("the approval store's consume path is the only status mutator", async () => {
   141	    const { readFileSync } = await import("node:fs");
   142	    const source = readFileSync(
   143	      join(process.cwd(), "src/governance/approvals/approval-store.ts"),
   144	      "utf-8",
   145	    );
   146	    // Consumption sets the terminal status. If a new writer appeared that set
   147	    // `consumed` without going through the claim, single use would be forgeable.
   148	    expect(source).toMatch(/consumeApproved/);
   149	    expect(source).toMatch(/status = "consumed"/);
   150	    // Counting the assignment sites keeps a second, competing writer visible.
   151	    const writers = source.match(/\.status\s*=\s*"consumed"/g) ?? [];
   152	    expect(writers.length).toBe(1);
   153	  });
   154	
   155	  it("an approval must be approved, unexpired, key-matched and invocation-matched", async () => {
   156	    const { readFileSync } = await import("node:fs");
   157	    const source = readFileSync(
   158	      join(process.cwd(), "src/governance/approvals/approval-store.ts"),
   159	      "utf-8",
   160	    );
   161	    // Each guard is a refusal that must exist independently: removing any one
   162	    // would let a stale or foreign approval authorize an invocation.
   163	    for (const guard of [
   164	      /status !== "approved"/,
   165	      /expiresAt\) <= now/,
   166	      /bindingKey !== expectedBindingKey/,
   167	      /invocationMatches/,
   168	    ]) {
   169	      expect(source, String(guard)).toMatch(guard);
   170	    }
   171	  });
   172	});
   173	
   174	// ── Control: durable pause on missing authority ───────────────────────────
   175	describe("missing authority is a pause, never an implicit execute", () => {
   176	  it("a write requiring approval is not allowed by a permissive capability grant alone", async () => {
   177	    const { PolicyGate, DEFAULT_CONFIG } = await defaultGate();
   178	    const config = structuredClone(DEFAULT_CONFIG) as never as {
   179	      permissions: { tools: Record<string, string> };
   180	    };
   181	    // Default `file.write` is `ask`; even under bypass the owned-path rule is
   182	    // what authorizes, so an owned write is scoped rather than unconditionally
   183	    // permitted.
   184	    const gate = new PolicyGate(config as never);
   185	    const decision = await gate.evaluateToolCall({
   186	      requestId: "ask-1",
   187	      toolName: "file.create",
   188	      capability: "file.write",
   189	      args: { path: "a.txt", content: "x" },
   190	      cwd: process.cwd(),
   191	      sessionMode: "ask",
   192	      source: "tool",
   193	      ownedPaths: ["a.txt"],
   194	    } as never);
   195	    // Owned writes are pre-authorized; the assertion is that the decision is
   196	    // attributable to the owned-path rule, not to a blanket allow.
   197	    expect(decision.matchedRuleId).toBe("owned-path-rule");
   198	  });

    65	function invocationFields(invocation: InvocationIdentity | undefined): {
    66	  invocation?: InvocationIdentity;
    67	  invocationFingerprint?: string;
    68	} {
    69	  return {
    70	    invocation,
    71	    invocationFingerprint: invocation ? computeInvocationFingerprint(invocation) : undefined,
    72	  };

    74	
    75	export class ApprovalStore {
    76	  private approvals: ApprovalRecord[] = [];
    77	  private groups: ApprovalGroup[] = [];
    78	  private filePath: string;
    79	  /** Append-only delta log for new records (#703); folded into the snapshot
    80	   *  on compaction. External readers must replay it after the snapshot. */
    81	  private journalPath: string;
    82	  private cwd: string;
    83	  private auditStore?: AuditStore;
    84	  private eventLog?: EventLog;
    85	  /** In-flight fire-and-forget appends, for a deterministic test barrier. */
    86	  private pendingAppends: Promise<unknown>[] = [];
    87	
    88	  /**
    89	   * Whether this store emits lifecycle events itself.
    90	   *
    91	   * `emit()` is a no-op without an EventLog, so a caller that constructs the
    92	   * store WITHOUT one (e.g. headless `alix run`, `run.ts`) gets no
    93	   * `approval.created` from the committed creation transition. The policy gate
    94	   * uses this to decide whether it must supply the creation record itself —
    95	   * exactly one emitter either way. Read-only projection of construction state;
    96	   * it grants no authority.
    97	   */
    98	  get emitsLifecycleEvents(): boolean {
    99	    return this.eventLog !== undefined;
   100	  }
   101	
   102	  /** O(1) lookup indexes (#703), rebuilt on load and after each mutation. */
   103	  private byId = new Map<string, ApprovalRecord>();
   104	  private byBindingKey = new Map<string, ApprovalRecord[]>();
   105	
   106	  /** Retention cap for terminal (non-pending/approved) records (#703). */
   107	  private readonly maxTerminalRecords: number;
   108	  /**
   109	   * R2.5 authority counters. The LEDGER append is the commit (append failure
   110	   * throws); the JSON/journal projection is best-effort — failures are
   111	   * counted here and surfaced by `ledgerStatus()` + reconciliation, never
   112	   * silent.
   113	   */
   114	  private readonly ledgerCounters: LedgerFactCounters = { appends: 0, failures: 0 };
   115	  private projectionFailures = 0;
   116	  private lastProjectionError: string | undefined;
   117	
   118	  constructor(cwd: string, opts?: { auditStore?: AuditStore; eventLog?: EventLog; maxTerminalRecords?: number }) {
   119	    this.cwd = cwd;
   120	    this.filePath = join(cwd, ".alix", "approvals", "approvals.json");
   121	    this.journalPath = `${this.filePath}.journal.jsonl`;
   122	    this.auditStore = opts?.auditStore;
   123	    this.eventLog = opts?.eventLog;
   124	    this.maxTerminalRecords = opts?.maxTerminalRecords ?? 1_000;
   125	  }
   126	
   127	  /** Rebuild the id / binding-key indexes from the in-memory record array. */
   128	  private rebuildIndexes(): void {
   129	    this.byId.clear();
   130	    this.byBindingKey.clear();
   131	    for (const record of this.approvals) {
   132	      this.byId.set(record.id, record);
   133	      const bucket = this.byBindingKey.get(record.bindingKey);
   134	      if (bucket) bucket.push(record);
   135	      else this.byBindingKey.set(record.bindingKey, [record]);
   136	    }
   137	  }
   138	
   139	  /**
   140	   * Bound growth (#703): drop the oldest terminal records beyond the
   141	   * retention cap. Pending/approved records are never pruned (they are
   142	   * still actionable). Returns true when anything was removed.
   143	   */
   144	  private pruneTerminal(): boolean {
   145	    const isTerminal = (s: ApprovalRecord["status"]): boolean =>
   146	      s === "denied" || s === "consumed" || s === "expired" || s === "revoked" || s === "invalidated";
   147	    const terminal = this.approvals.filter((r) => isTerminal(r.status));
   148	    if (terminal.length <= this.maxTerminalRecords) return false;
   149	    // createdAt is ISO-8601, so lexicographic order is chronological.
   150	    terminal.sort((a, b) => a.createdAt.localeCompare(b.createdAt));
   151	    const drop = new Set(terminal.slice(0, terminal.length - this.maxTerminalRecords).map((r) => r.id));
   152	    this.approvals = this.approvals.filter((r) => !drop.has(r.id));
   153	    return true;
   154	  }
   155	
   156	  /**
   157	   * Load approvals — LEDGER first (R2.5 authority). In-memory state is
   158	   * rebuilt from the latest ledger fact per approval; the JSON projection
   159	   * (snapshot + journal) is consulted only for legacy records with zero
   160	   * ledger facts. Groups remain projection-only. Ledger db errors THROW —
   350	  /**
   351	   * Acquire the per-file lock, load fresh, run a mutation, then persist.
   352	   * Pure additions append to the journal (no snapshot rewrite); updates,
   353	   * deletions, or retention pruning compact the snapshot (#703).
   354	   */
   355	  async mutate<T>(fn: (approvals: ApprovalRecord[]) => T | Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
   356	    const lock = new ApprovalStoreLock(this.cwd);
   357	    const acquired = await lock.acquire();
   358	    if (!acquired) throw new Error("Could not acquire approval lock");
   359	    try {
   360	      await this.load();
   361	      const before = new Map<string, string>();
   362	      for (const r of this.approvals) before.set(r.id, JSON.stringify(r));
   363	
   364	      const result = await fn(this.approvals);
   365	
   366	      // Classify the diff: additions vs updates/deletions.
   367	      const added: ApprovalRecord[] = [];
   368	      let mutatedExisting = false;
   369	      for (const r of this.approvals) {
   370	        const prior = before.get(r.id);
   371	        if (prior === undefined) added.push(r);
   372	        else if (prior !== JSON.stringify(r)) mutatedExisting = true;
   373	      }
   374	      const afterIds = new Set(this.approvals.map((r) => r.id));
   375	      for (const id of before.keys()) {
   376	        if (!afterIds.has(id)) { mutatedExisting = true; break; }
   377	      }
   378	
   379	      const pruned = this.pruneTerminal();
   380	      this.rebuildIndexes();
   381	
   382	      // R2.5 authority: the ledger append IS the commit — it runs BEFORE the
   383	      // projection and throws on failure (no JSON-only state can exist).
   384	      const finalIds = new Set(this.approvals.map((r) => r.id));
   385	      for (const r of this.approvals) {
   386	        const prior = before.get(r.id);
   387	        if (prior === undefined) this.appendApprovalFact(r, "created");
   388	        else if (prior !== JSON.stringify(r)) this.appendApprovalFact(r, "updated");
   389	      }
   390	      for (const id of before.keys()) {
   391	        if (!finalIds.has(id)) this.appendApprovalRemoved(id);
   392	      }
   393	
   394	      // Projection second: failure tolerated and counted — the ledger holds
   395	      // the truth and reconciliation reports projection drift for rebuild.
   396	      try {
   397	        if (!mutatedExisting && !pruned && added.length > 0 && existsSync(this.filePath)) {
   398	          // Append-only fast path: no unrelated history rewritten. (The first
   399	          // write compacts so `approvals.json` exists for external readers.)
   400	          await this.appendJournal(added);
   401	          const journalLines = await this.journalLineCount();
   402	          if (journalLines >= JOURNAL_COMPACT_THRESHOLD) {
   403	            await this.saveAtomic();
   404	          }
   405	        } else {
   406	          await this.saveAtomic();
   407	        }
   408	      } catch (err) {
   409	        this.projectionFailures += 1;
   410	        this.lastProjectionError = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
   411	      }
   412	
   413	      return result;
   414	    } finally {
   415	      lock.release();
   416	    }
   417	  }
   418	
   419	  /** Count journal lines (cheap; used to trigger compaction). */
   420	  private async journalLineCount(): Promise<number> {
   421	    if (!existsSync(this.journalPath)) return 0;
   422	    try {
   423	      const raw = await readFile(this.journalPath, "utf-8");
   424	      return raw.split("\n").filter(Boolean).length;
   425	    } catch {
   426	      return 0;
   427	    }
   428	  }
   429	
   430	  // ─── Enriched request types ───────────────────────────────────────

    35	COMMAND_ROUTER["tui"] = async () => {
    36	  const { runTui } = await import("./interfaces/cli/commands/tui.js");
    37	  return { handler: async (a) => {
    38	    const modeIdx = a.indexOf("--mode");
    39	    const sessionMode = modeIdx >= 0 ? a[modeIdx + 1] as "auto" | "ask" | "bypass" : undefined;
    40	    const daemonMode = a.includes("--daemon");
    41	    const themeIdx = a.indexOf("--theme");
    42	    const themeName = themeIdx >= 0 ? a[themeIdx + 1] : undefined;
    43	    const rendererFlag = a.find((arg) => arg === "--renderer" || arg.startsWith("--renderer="));
    44	    const rendererIdx = a.indexOf("--renderer");
    45	    const renderer = rendererFlag?.startsWith("--renderer=")
    46	      ? rendererFlag.slice("--renderer=".length)
    47	      : rendererIdx >= 0 ? a[rendererIdx + 1] : undefined;
    48	    if (rendererFlag !== undefined && !renderer) {
    49	      console.error("Missing TUI renderer after --renderer. Supported: canvas, opentui.");
    50	      return 1;
    51	    }
    52	    if (renderer && renderer !== "canvas" && renderer !== "opentui") {
    53	      console.error(`Unknown TUI renderer '${renderer}'. Supported: canvas, opentui.`);
    54	      return 1;
    55	    }
    56	    try {
    57	      await runTui({ sessionMode, daemonMode, themeName, renderer: renderer as "canvas" | "opentui" | undefined });
    58	    } catch (error) {
    59	      if (renderer !== "opentui") throw error;
    60	      console.error(error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error));
    61	      return 1;
    62	    }
    63	    return 0;
    64	  }};

import { describe, it } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert";
import { CommandClassifier } from "../../src/governance/policy/command-classifier.js";
describe("CommandClassifier", () => {
    const classifier = new CommandClassifier();
    it("classifies safe read-only commands", () => {
        const result = classifier.classify("cat src/index.ts");
        assert.equal(result.risk, "low");
        assert.ok(result.safe);
    });
    it("classifies git commands", () => {
        const result = classifier.classify("git status");
        assert.equal(result.risk, "medium");
        assert.ok(!result.safe);
    });
    it("classifies destructive commands", () => {
        const result = classifier.classify("rm -rf node_modules");
        assert.equal(result.risk, "high");
        assert.ok(!result.safe);
        assert.ok(result.tags.includes("destructive") || result.tags.includes("file-modification"));
    });
    it("extracts file paths from commands", () => {
        const result = classifier.classify("git add src/*.ts");
        assert.ok(result.paths.some(p => p.includes("src")));
    });
    it("classifies network commands", () => {
        const result = classifier.classify("curl https://api.example.com");
        assert.ok(result.networkDestination);
        assert.equal(result.networkDestination, "api.example.com");
    });
    it("classifies npm/yarn commands", () => {
        const result = classifier.classify("npm install");
        assert.equal(result.risk, "medium");
        assert.ok(result.tags.includes("dependency"));
    });
    it("classifies shell operators", () => {
        const result = classifier.classify("echo 'hi' && ls");
        assert.ok(result.hasChain);
    });
});

 * rule-evaluator.test.ts — Tests for RuleEvaluator and default policies.

import { describe, it } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { RuleEvaluator } from "../../src/governance/policy/rule-evaluator.js";
import { defaultPolicyRules } from "../../src/governance/policy/default-policies.js";
import { validatePolicyRule } from "../../src/governance/policy/policy-rule.js";
describe("RuleEvaluator", () => {
    it("evaluates first matching rule", () => {
        const engine = new RuleEvaluator([
            { id: "first", description: "First", match: { capability: "a" }, decision: "allow", enabled: true },
            { id: "second", description: "Second", match: { capability: "b" }, decision: "deny", enabled: true },
        ]);
        const result = engine.evaluate({ capability: "b" });
        assert.equal(result.decision, "deny");
        assert.equal(result.matchedRuleId, "second");
    });
    it('falls back to "deny" when no rule matches', () => {
        const engine = new RuleEvaluator([]);
        const result = engine.evaluate({ capability: "unknown" });
        assert.equal(result.decision, "deny");
        assert.equal(result.reason, "No matching policy rule; denied by default");
    });
    it("skips disabled rules", () => {
        const engine = new RuleEvaluator([
            { id: "disabled", description: "Disabled", match: { capability: "x" }, decision: "allow", enabled: false },
            { id: "fallback", description: "Fallback", match: {}, decision: "deny", enabled: true },
        ]);
        const result = engine.evaluate({ capability: "x" });
        assert.equal(result.matchedRuleId, "fallback");
    });
    it("supports batch evaluation", () => {

   492	  /**
   493	   * requestFresh — Always creates a new approval record even if one with the
   494	   * same binding key already exists. Delegates to mutate() for thread safety.
   495	   */
   496	  async requestFresh(input: ApprovalRequestInput): Promise<ApprovalRecord> {
   497	    return this.createApprovalRecord(input);
   498	  }
   499	
   500	  /**
   501	   * requestOrReusePending — Atomic lookup + insert inside a single mutate() call.
   502	   *
   503	   * If a pending approval with the same bindingKey already exists, returns it.
   504	   * Otherwise creates a new one via the same logic as requestBound.
   505	   *
   506	   * This eliminates the check-then-create race that requestBound() leaves to callers.
   507	   */
   508	  async requestOrReusePending(input: ApprovalRequestInput): Promise<ApprovalRecord> {
   509	    return this.mutate((approvals) => {
   510	      // Look for existing pending approval with the same binding key
   511	      const existing = approvals.find(
   512	        (a) => a.status === "pending" && a.bindingKey === input.bindingKey,
   513	      );
   514	      if (existing) {
   515	        return { ...existing };
   516	      }
   517	
   518	      // No existing pending — create a new record
   519	      const record: ApprovalRecord = {
   520	        id: `approval_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`,
   521	        schemaVersion: "2.0",
   522	        status: "pending",
   523	        usePolicy: "single_use",
   524	        bindingKey: input.bindingKey,
   525	        requestFingerprint: input.requestFingerprint,
   526	        policyRevision: input.policyRevision,
   527	        capabilities: input.capabilities,
   528	        ownershipClaims: input.ownershipClaims ?? [],
   529	        reason: input.reason,
   530	        createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
   531	        expiresAt: input.expiresAt ?? new Date(Date.now() + 30 * 60_000).toISOString(),
   532	        coordinationRunId: input.coordinationRunId,
   533	        workerId: input.workerId,
   534	        workerAttempt: input.workerAttempt,
   535	        graphId: input.graphId,
   760	      if (a.requestId !== requestId) continue;
   761	      if (!latest || a.createdAt > latest.createdAt) latest = a;
   762	    }
   763	    return latest;
   764	  }

   766	  /** Find an approval record by exact binding key — O(1) via index (#703). */
   767	  findExact(bindingKey: string): ApprovalRecord | undefined {

   770	
   771	  /**
   772	   * Record what happened after a claim (`succeeded` | `failed` | `cancelled`).
   773	   *
   774	   * Best-effort by contract: the tool result must never be reclassified because
   775	   * outcome persistence failed, so callers ignore the boolean. Only the first
   776	   * terminal transition wins — a stale or duplicated report cannot rewrite a
   777	   * claim that another execution already resolved.
   778	   */
   779	  async recordExecutionOutcome(
   780	    id: string,
   781	    outcome: "succeeded" | "failed" | "cancelled",
   782	  ): Promise<boolean> {
   783	    let recorded = false;
   784	    let sessionId: string | undefined;
   785	    await this.mutate((approvals) => {
   786	      const r = approvals.find(a => a.id === id);
   787	      if (!r) return;
   788	      if (r.status !== "consumed") return;
   789	      if (r.executionOutcome !== "pending") return;
   790	      r.executionOutcome = outcome;
   791	      r.executionOutcomeAt = new Date().toISOString();
   792	      sessionId = r.sessionId;
   793	      recorded = true;
   794	    });
   795	    if (recorded) {
   796	      const type = outcome === "succeeded"
   797	        ? APPROVAL_EVENT_TYPES.EXECUTION_SUCCEEDED
   798	        : outcome === "cancelled"
   799	          ? APPROVAL_EVENT_TYPES.EXECUTION_CANCELLED
   800	          : APPROVAL_EVENT_TYPES.EXECUTION_FAILED;
   801	      this.emit(type, { approvalId: id, outcome }, sessionId);
   802	    }
   803	    return recorded;
   804	  }
   805	
   806	  /**
   807	   * Classify a `pending` claim as `unverified`: the process that held it is
   808	   * gone, so the side effect is unknown. Terminal and fail-closed — the
   809	   * approval is never reissued by this transition.
   810	   *
   811	   * Returns the record's current state so a caller can name the exact reason.
   812	   */
   813	  async markExecutionUnverified(id: string): Promise<"unverified" | "terminal" | "missing"> {
   814	    let verdict: "unverified" | "terminal" | "missing" = "missing";
   815	    let sessionId: string | undefined;
   816	    await this.mutate((approvals) => {
   817	      const r = approvals.find(a => a.id === id);
   818	      if (!r) { verdict = "missing"; return; }
   819	      if (r.status !== "consumed") { verdict = "terminal"; return; }
   820	      if (r.executionOutcome === "unverified") { verdict = "unverified"; return; }

   146	 */
   147	export function createWorkerApprovalPort(
   148	  store: ApprovalStore,
   149	  options: { workerId?: string } = {},
   150	): WorkerApprovalPort {
   151	  const boundWorkerId = options.workerId;
   152	  const port: WorkerApprovalPort = {
   153	    get: (id) => store.get(id),
   154	    loadFresh: (id) => store.loadFresh(id),
   155	    findExact: (bindingKey) => store.findExact(bindingKey),
   156	    findPendingByBindingKey: (bindingKey) => store.findPendingByBindingKey(bindingKey),
   157	    findPending: (query) => store.findPending(query),
   158	    findResolved: (opts) => store.findResolved(opts),
   159	    findByRequestId: (requestId) => store.findByRequestId(requestId),
   160	    request: (opts) => store.request(opts),
   161	    requestOrReusePending: (input) => store.requestOrReusePending(input),
   162	    consumeApproved: store.consumeApproved.bind(store),
   163	    recordExecutionOutcome: async (id, outcome) => {
   164	      // Ownership check FIRST: a worker may only report on a record it owns, or
   165	      // on a legacy record with no worker binding. A port constructed without a
   166	      // workerId therefore refuses to report on worker-bound records at all
   167	      // (fail closed) while still serving legacy records.
   168	      const record = store.get(id) ?? await store.loadFresh(id);
   169	      if (!record) return false;
   170	      if (record.workerId && record.workerId !== boundWorkerId) return false;
   171	      return store.recordExecutionOutcome(id, outcome);
   172	    },
   173	    load: () => store.load(),
   174	    markExecutionUnverified: (id) => store.markExecutionUnverified(id),
   175	  };
   176	
   177	  // Structural guarantee, not a convention: no forbidden method is reachable
   178	  // from the object a worker receives. A future refactor that added one would
   179	  // fail this check immediately.
   180	  for (const method of FORBIDDEN) {
   181	    if (method in port) {
   182	      throw new Error(`WorkerApprovalPort must not expose "${method}"`);
   183	    }
   184	  }
   185	  return port;

     2	 * rule-evaluator.test.ts — Tests for RuleEvaluator and default policies.

     5	import { describe, it } from "node:test";
     6	import assert from "node:assert/strict";
     7	import { RuleEvaluator } from "../../src/governance/policy/rule-evaluator.js";
     8	import { defaultPolicyRules } from "../../src/governance/policy/default-policies.js";
     9	import { validatePolicyRule } from "../../src/governance/policy/policy-rule.js";
    10	
    11	describe("RuleEvaluator", () => {
    12	  it("evaluates first matching rule", () => {
    13	    const engine = new RuleEvaluator([
    14	      { id: "first", description: "First", match: { capability: "a" }, decision: "allow", enabled: true },
    15	      { id: "second", description: "Second", match: { capability: "b" }, decision: "deny", enabled: true },
    16	    ]);
    17	    const result = engine.evaluate({ capability: "b" });
    18	    assert.equal(result.decision, "deny");
    19	    assert.equal(result.matchedRuleId, "second");
    20	  });
    21	
    22	  it('falls back to "deny" when no rule matches', () => {
    23	    const engine = new RuleEvaluator([]);
    24	    const result = engine.evaluate({ capability: "unknown" });
    25	    assert.equal(result.decision, "deny");
    26	    assert.equal(result.reason, "No matching policy rule; denied by default");
    27	  });
    28	
    29	  it("skips disabled rules", () => {
    30	    const engine = new RuleEvaluator([
    31	      { id: "disabled", description: "Disabled", match: { capability: "x" }, decision: "allow", enabled: false },
    32	      { id: "fallback", description: "Fallback", match: {}, decision: "deny", enabled: true },
    33	    ]);
    34	    const result = engine.evaluate({ capability: "x" });
    35	    assert.equal(result.matchedRuleId, "fallback");
    36	  });
    37	
    38	  it("supports batch evaluation", () => {
    39	    const engine = new RuleEvaluator([
    40	      { id: "allow-a", description: "Allow a", match: { capability: "a" }, decision: "allow", enabled: true },
    41	      { id: "deny-b", description: "Deny b", match: { capability: "b" }, decision: "deny", enabled: true },
    42	    ]);
    43	    const results = engine.evaluateBatch([
    44	      { capability: "a" },
    45	      { capability: "b" },
    46	      { capability: "c" },
    47	    ]);
    48	    assert.equal(results.length, 3);
    49	    assert.equal(results[0].decision, "allow");
    50	    assert.equal(results[1].decision, "deny");
    51	    assert.equal(results[2].decision, "deny");
    52	  });
    53	
    54	  it("addRule appends to the rule list", () => {
    55	    const engine = new RuleEvaluator();
    56	    engine.addRule({
    57	      id: "added", description: "Added later", match: {}, decision: "allow", enabled: true,
    58	    });
    59	    assert.equal(engine.getAllRules().length, 1);
    60	  });
    61	
    62	  it("setRules replaces all rules", () => {
    63	    const engine = new RuleEvaluator([
    64	      { id: "old", description: "Old", match: {}, decision: "deny", enabled: true },
    65	    ]);
    66	    engine.setRules([
    67	      { id: "new", description: "New", match: {}, decision: "allow", enabled: true },
    68	    ]);
    69	    assert.equal(engine.getAllRules().length, 1);
    70	    assert.equal(engine.getAllRules()[0].id, "new");
    71	  });
    72	
    73	  it("getEnabledRules excludes disabled", () => {
    74	    const engine = new RuleEvaluator([
    75	      { id: "a", description: "A", match: {}, decision: "allow", enabled: true },
    76	      { id: "b", description: "B", match: {}, decision: "deny", enabled: false },
    77	    ]);
    78	    assert.equal(engine.getEnabledRules().length, 1);
    79	  });
    80	});
    81	
    82	describe("Default policies", () => {
    83	  it("returns expected number of default rules", () => {
    84	    const rules = defaultPolicyRules();
    85	    assert.ok(rules.length >= 8);
    86	  });
    87	
    88	  it("all rules pass validation", () => {
    89	    const rules = defaultPolicyRules();
    90	    for (const rule of rules) {
    91	      const result = validatePolicyRule(rule);
    92	      if (!result.valid) {
    93	        assert.fail(`Rule ${rule.id} failed validation: ${result.errors.join("; ")}`);
    94	      }
    95	    }
    96	  });
    97	
    98	  it("evaluates web.search as allow", () => {
    99	    const evaluator = new RuleEvaluator(defaultPolicyRules());
   100	    const result = evaluator.evaluate({ capability: "web.search", riskLevel: "low" });
   101	    assert.equal(result.decision, "allow");
   102	  });
   103	
   104	  it("evaluates shell.exec as ask", () => {

   107	    assert.equal(result.decision, "ask");
   108	  });
   109	
   110	  it("evaluates unknown capability as deny", () => {
   111	    const evaluator = new RuleEvaluator(defaultPolicyRules());
   112	    const result = evaluator.evaluate({ capability: "nonexistent.magic", riskLevel: "critical" });
   113	    assert.equal(result.decision, "deny");
   114	  });
   115

   117	    const evaluator = new RuleEvaluator(defaultPolicyRules());
   118	    const result = evaluator.evaluate({ riskLevel: "low" });
   119	    assert.equal(result.decision, "allow");

   620	  /** Legacy request wrapper — generates a safe binding from available fields. */
   621	  async request(opts: {
   622	    reason: string;
   623	    graphId?: string;
   624	    nodeId?: string;
   625	    sessionId?: string;
   626	    agentId?: string;
   627	    capability?: string;
   628	    toolId?: string;
   629	    riskLevel?: "low" | "medium" | "high" | "critical";
   630	    /** Exact invocation this approval authorizes; see `ApprovalRequestInput`. */
   631	    invocation?: InvocationIdentity;
   632	    /**
   633	     * Stable request id (typically the toolCallId) that lets the policy
   634	     * gate recognise a re-execution of the SAME tool call. When the gate
   635	     * sees a previously-resolved approval with this requestId, it can
   636	     * honour that resolution instead of creating a duplicate approval.
   637	     */
   638	    requestId?: string;
   639	  }): Promise<ApprovalRecord> {
   640	    const { computeBindingKey } = await import("./approval-binding.js");
   641	    const requestFingerprint = `legacy:${opts.capability ?? "unknown"}:${opts.graphId ?? ""}:${opts.nodeId ?? ""}:${opts.sessionId ?? ""}`;
   642	    const bindingKey = computeBindingKey({
   643	      capabilities: opts.capability ? [opts.capability] : [],
   644	      ownershipClaims: [],
   645	      requestFingerprint,
   646	      policyRevision: "legacy",
   647	      graphId: opts.graphId,
   648	      nodeId: opts.nodeId,
   649	      sessionId: opts.sessionId,
   650	    });
   651	    return this.requestBound({
   652	      reason: opts.reason,
   653	      bindingKey,
   654	      requestFingerprint,
   655	      policyRevision: "legacy",
   656	      capabilities: opts.capability ? [opts.capability] : [],
   657	      graphId: opts.graphId,
   658	      nodeId: opts.nodeId,
   659	      sessionId: opts.sessionId,
   660	      agentId: opts.agentId,
   661	      toolId: opts.toolId,
   662	      riskLevel: opts.riskLevel,
   663	      invocation: opts.invocation,
   664	      requestId: opts.requestId,
   665	    });
   666	  }

   874	  /**
   875	   * Mark all pending or approved approvals past their expiry as expired.
   876	   * Returns the list of newly expired records.
   877	   */
   878	  async expireDue(now?: Date): Promise<ApprovalRecord[]> {
   879	    const cutoff = now ?? new Date();
   880	    const expired: ApprovalRecord[] = [];
   881	    await this.mutate((approvals) => {
   882	      for (const r of approvals) {
   883	        if ((r.status === "pending" || r.status === "approved") && new Date(r.expiresAt) <= cutoff) {
   884	          r.status = "expired";
   885	          expired.push({ ...r });
   886	        }
   887	      }
   888	    });
   889	    for (const r of expired) {
   890	      this.emit(APPROVAL_EVENT_TYPES.EXPIRED, { approvalId: r.id }, r.sessionId);
   891	    }
   892	    return expired;
   893	  }

   895	  /**
   896	   * Revoke an approval. Terminal states (consumed, expired) cannot be revoked.
   897	   */
   898	  async revoke(id: string, context: { actor: string; reason: string; now?: Date }): Promise<ApprovalRecord | null> {
   899	    let revoked: ApprovalRecord | null = null;
   900	    await this.mutate((approvals) => {
   901	      const r = approvals.find(a => a.id === id);
   902	      if (!r || r.status === "consumed" || r.status === "expired") return;
   903	      r.status = "revoked";
   904	      r.revokedAt = (context.now ?? new Date()).toISOString();
   905	      r.revokedBy = context.actor;
   906	      r.revocationReason = context.reason;
   907	      revoked = { ...r };
   908	    });
   909	    // `revoked` is assigned inside the mutate() closure, so TS keeps the initial
   910	    // `null` narrowing here (closure assignments don't widen outer flow). Re-assert.
   911	    const revokedRecord = revoked as ApprovalRecord | null;
   912	    if (revokedRecord) {
   913	      this.emit(APPROVAL_EVENT_TYPES.REVOKED, { approvalId: revokedRecord.id }, revokedRecord.sessionId);
   914	    }
   915	    return revoked;
   916	  }

     1	/**
     2	 * approval-store-lock.ts — Per-file lock for ApprovalStore.
     3	 *
     4	 * Uses atomic mkdir for locking. Lock stored at .alix/approvals/approvals.lock.
     5	 * Token-safe release, stale PID recovery.
     6	 */
     7	
     8	import { mkdirSync, rmSync, writeFileSync, readFileSync, existsSync } from "node:fs";
     9	import { join, dirname } from "node:path";
    10	import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
    11	
    12	const STALE_LOCK_MS = 60_000;
    13	const POLL_INTERVAL_MS = 50;
    14	const DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS = 10_000;
    15	
    16	export type ApprovalLockMetadata = {
    17	  pid: number;
    18	  token: string;
    19	  acquiredAt: string;
    20	};
    21	
    22	export class ApprovalStoreLock {
    23	  private readonly lockPath: string;
    24	  private readonly token: string;
    25	  private acquired = false;
    26	
    27	  constructor(cwd: string) {
    28	    this.lockPath = join(cwd, ".alix", "approvals", "approvals.lock");
    29	    this.token = randomUUID();
    30	  }
    31	
    32	  async acquire(timeoutMs = DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS): Promise<boolean> {
    33	    if (this.acquired) return true;
    34	    const deadline = Date.now() + timeoutMs;
    35	    mkdirSync(dirname(this.lockPath), { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
    36	
    37	    while (Date.now() < deadline) {
    38	      try {
    39	        mkdirSync(this.lockPath);
    40	        writeFileSync(
    41	          join(this.lockPath, "meta.json"),
    42	          JSON.stringify({ pid: process.pid, token: this.token, acquiredAt: new Date().toISOString() } as ApprovalLockMetadata),
    43	          "utf-8",
    44	        );
    45	        this.acquired = true;
    46	        return true;
    47	      } catch {
    48	        if (this.isStale()) {
    49	          rmSync(this.lockPath, { recursive: true, force: true });
    50	          continue;
    51	        }
    52	      }
    53	      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, POLL_INTERVAL_MS));
    54	    }
    55	    return false;
    56	  }
    57	
    58	  release(): void {
    59	    if (!this.acquired) return;
    60	    try {
    61	      if (existsSync(this.lockPath)) {
    62	        const metaPath = join(this.lockPath, "meta.json");
    63	        if (existsSync(metaPath)) {
    64	          const saved = JSON.parse(readFileSync(metaPath, "utf-8")) as ApprovalLockMetadata;
    65	          if (saved.token === this.token) {
    66	            rmSync(this.lockPath, { recursive: true, force: true });
    67	          }
    68	        }
    69	      }
    70	    } catch { /* best-effort */ }
    71	    this.acquired = false;
    72	  }
    73	
    74	  isHeld(): boolean { return this.acquired; }
    75	
    76	  private isStale(): boolean {
    77	    try {
    78	      const metaPath = join(this.lockPath, "meta.json");
    79	      if (!existsSync(metaPath)) return false;
    80	      const saved = JSON.parse(readFileSync(metaPath, "utf-8")) as ApprovalLockMetadata;
    81	      if (Date.now() - new Date(saved.acquiredAt).getTime() < STALE_LOCK_MS) return false;
    82	      try { process.kill(saved.pid, 0); return false; } catch { return true; }
    83	    } catch { return false; }
    84	  }
    85	}
   280	   *  intentionally non-fatal); it exists so tests can assert the emitted
   281	   *  lifecycle events without racing the async append. */
   282	  async flushEvents(): Promise<void> {
   283	    const pending = this.pendingAppends.splice(0);
   284	    await Promise.all(pending);
   285	  }
   286	
   287	  /** Compact: write the full snapshot atomically, then clear the journal. */
   288	  private async saveAtomic(): Promise<void> {
   289	    const dir = join(this.filePath, "..");
   290	    if (!existsSync(dir)) {
   291	      await mkdir(dir, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
   292	    }
   293	    const token = randomUUID().slice(0, 8);
   294	    const tmpPath = `${this.filePath}.tmp.${token}`;
   295	    const state = { approvals: this.approvals, groups: this.groups ?? [] };
   296	    await writeFile(tmpPath, JSON.stringify(state, null, 2), { encoding: "utf-8", mode: 0o600, flag: "wx" });
   297	    await renameFile(tmpPath, this.filePath);
   298	    // Snapshot now includes every journal record — drop the delta.
   299	    await rm(this.journalPath, { force: true }).catch(() => {});
   300	  }

   305	   * never leave a JSON-only mutation behind.
   306	   */
   307	  private appendApprovalFact(record: ApprovalRecord, kind: "created" | "updated"): void {
   308	    const expected = currentEntityVersion(this.cwd, this.ledgerCounters, record.id);
   309	    appendFact(this.cwd, this.ledgerCounters, {

   317	      actor: { type: "system", id: "approval-store" },

   320	      errorLabel: "approval ledger",
   321	    });
   322	  }
   323	
   324	  /** R2.5 append a removal tombstone (prune or delete) — the commit. Throws on failure. */
   325	  private appendApprovalRemoved(id: string): void {
   326	    appendFact(this.cwd, this.ledgerCounters, {
   327	      eventType: "approval.removed",
   328	      entityType: "approval",
   329	      entityId: id,
   330	      payload: { removed: true },
   940	   * Atomically consume a single-use approved approval.
   941	   * Validates: status, expiry, binding key match.
   942	   */
   943	  async consumeApproved(
   944	    id: string,
   945	    expectedBindingKey: string,
   946	    consumer: { workerId?: string; workerAttempt?: number; now?: Date },
   947	    expectedInvocation?: InvocationIdentity,
   948	  ): Promise<ConsumeResult> {
   949	    let result: ConsumeResult = { consumed: false, reason: "not found" };
   950	    await this.mutate((approvals) => {
   951	      const r = approvals.find(a => a.id === id);
   952	      if (!r) { result = { consumed: false, reason: "not found" }; return; }
   953	      if (r.status !== "approved") { result = { consumed: false, reason: `status is ${r.status}` }; return; }
   954	      const now = consumer.now ?? new Date();
   955	      if (new Date(r.expiresAt) <= now) { result = { consumed: false, reason: "expired" }; return; }
   956	      if (r.bindingKey !== expectedBindingKey) { result = { consumed: false, reason: "binding key mismatch" }; return; }
   957	      if (r.workerId && consumer.workerId && r.workerId !== consumer.workerId) { result = { consumed: false, reason: "worker mismatch" }; return; }
   958	      // Exact-invocation binding: a caller that supplies the identity must match
   959	      // the persisted fingerprint. A legacy record with no fingerprint fails
   960	      // closed — it is never reinterpreted as reusable.
   961	      if (expectedInvocation && !invocationMatches(r, expectedInvocation)) {
   962	        result = { consumed: false, reason: "invocation mismatch" }; return;
   963	      }
   964	      r.status = "consumed";
   965	      r.consumedAt = (consumer.now ?? new Date()).toISOString();
   966	      r.consumedByWorkerId = consumer.workerId;
   967	      r.consumedAttempt = consumer.workerAttempt;
   968	      // The claim is durable; execution follows and reports its own outcome.
   969	      // A crash in between leaves this `pending`, which a later replay
   970	      // classifies as `unverified` rather than silently reissuing authority.
   971	      r.executionOutcome = "pending";
   972	      r.executionOutcomeAt = r.consumedAt;
   973	      result = { consumed: true, record: { ...r } };
   974	    });
   975	    // `result` is assigned inside the mutate() closure, so TS narrows it to the
   976	    // initial `{ consumed: false }` variant here. Re-assert the declared type.
   977	    const consumeOutcome = result as ConsumeResult;
   978	    if (consumeOutcome.consumed && consumeOutcome.record) {
   979	      this.emit(APPROVAL_EVENT_TYPES.CONSUMED, { approvalId: consumeOutcome.record.id }, consumeOutcome.record.sessionId);
   980	    }
   981	    return result;
   982	  }

  1005	  /**
  1006	   * Create an approval group linking multiple approval records.
  1007	   * All approvals must share the same run, worker, attempt, scope hash,
  1008	   * policy revision, and risk level.
  1009	   */
  1010	  async createGroup(input: {
  1011	    approvalIds: string[];
  1012	    coordinationRunId?: string;
  1013	    workerId?: string;
  1014	    workerAttempt?: number;
  1015	    policyRevision: string;
  1016	    riskLevel?: "low" | "medium" | "high" | "critical";
  1017	    now?: Date;
  1018	  }): Promise<ApprovalGroup | null> {
  1019	    const now = input.now ?? new Date();
  1020	    const approvals = this.approvals.filter(a => input.approvalIds.includes(a.id));
  1021	
  1022	    // Validate all exist and are pending
  1023	    if (approvals.length !== input.approvalIds.length) return null;
  1024	    if (approvals.some(a => a.status !== "pending")) return null;
  1025	
  1026	    // Validate compatibility
  1027	    const first = approvals[0];
  1028	    if (approvals.some(a =>
  1029	      a.coordinationRunId !== first.coordinationRunId ||
  1030	      a.workerId !== first.workerId ||
  1031	      a.policyRevision !== first.policyRevision
  1032	    )) return null;
  1033	
  1034	    const group: ApprovalGroup = {
  1035	      id: `group_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`,
  1036	      schemaVersion: "1.0",
  1037	      approvalIds: [...input.approvalIds],
  1038	      coordinationRunId: input.coordinationRunId,
  1039	      workerId: input.workerId,
  1040	      workerAttempt: input.workerAttempt,
  1041	      policyRevision: input.policyRevision,
  1042	      riskLevel: input.riskLevel,
  1043	      status: "pending",
  1044	      createdAt: now.toISOString(),
  1045	    };
  1046	
  1047	    // Link approvals to group
  1048	    for (const a of approvals) {
  1049	      a.groupId = group.id;
  1050	    }
  1051	
  1052	    // Store groups alongside approvals
  1053	    this.groups ??= [];
  1054	    this.groups.push(group);
  1055	    await this.save();
  1056	    return group;
  1057	  }
  1058

  1064	  async resolveGroup(

   464	      });
   465	    }
   466	    if (rows.length < pageSize) return { events, truncated: false };
   467	  }
   468	  return { events, truncated: true };
   469	}

   471	/** Mutable append accounting a domain owns (module- or instance-scoped). */
   472	export interface LedgerFactCounters {
   473	  appends: number;
   474	  failures: number;
   475	  lastError?: string;
   476	}
   477	
   478	export interface AppendFactInput {
   479	  eventType: string;
   480	  entityType: string;
   481	  entityId: string;
   482	  payload: unknown;
   483	  correlationId: string;
   484	  actor: RuntimeEvent["actor"];

   492	  /** Optimistic precondition; default = current committed version. */
   493	  expectedVersion?: number;
   494	  /** Version to write; default `expectedVersion + 1` (immutable appends pass 1). */
   495	  entityVersion?: number;
   496	  /** Error-message prefix, e.g. "approval ledger". */
   497	  errorLabel: string;
   498	}
   499	
   500	/**
   501	 * Append one domain fact — THE COMMIT. Builds the R2 envelope, applies the
   502	 * optimistic precondition, records the outcome on `counters` (appends on
   503	 * success; failures + lastError on any failure), and throws on failure so an
   504	 * unavailable ledger can never leave a JSON-only mutation behind.
   505	 */
   506	/**
   507	 * Read the current committed entity version, accounting a db-open/read
   508	 * failure on `counters` and rethrowing. Use where the event type depends on
   509	 * whether the entity already exists (callers still call `appendFact`).
   510	 */
   511	export function currentEntityVersion(cwd: string, counters: LedgerFactCounters, entityId: string): number {
   512	  try {
   513	    return getSharedLedger(cwd).entityVersion(entityId);
   514	  } catch (err) {
   515	    counters.failures += 1;
   516	    counters.lastError = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
   517	    throw err;
   518	  }
   519	}

     1	/**
     2	 * approval-crash-recovery.test.ts — R1.5 crash-after-consume contract.
     3	 *
     4	 * Consumption and execution cannot be one transaction across a process
     5	 * boundary, so a crash between them is a real state. The contract pinned here:
     6	 *
     7	 *  - a claim is durable and marks the record `executionOutcome: "pending"`;
     8	 *  - a completed execution reports `succeeded` / `failed` / `cancelled`;
     9	 *  - a replay that observes a `pending` claim classifies it `unverified` and
    10	 *    DENIES — the side effect is unknown, so authority is never reissued and
    11	 *    the invocation is never retried automatically;
    12	 *  - uncertainty is listable for an operator without any wall-clock sweep.
    13	 *
    14	 * Every assertion reads real record state and real file effects, not just the
    15	 * returned verdict.
    16	 */
    17	
    18	import { describe, it } from "node:test";
    19	import assert from "node:assert/strict";
    20	import { mkdtempSync, rmSync, mkdirSync, readFileSync, existsSync } from "node:fs";
    21	import { join } from "node:path";
    22	import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
    23	import { createToolExecutor } from "../../src/capabilities/tools/tool-executor-factory.js";
    24	import type { ToolExecutor } from "../../src/capabilities/tools/tool-executor-factory.js";
    25	import { DEFAULT_CONFIG } from "../../src/operations/config/defaults.js";
    26	import { ApprovalStore } from "../../src/governance/approvals/approval-store.js";
    27	import { EventLog } from "../../src/runtime-state/events/event-log.js";
    28	import { ContinuationStore } from "../../src/runtime-state/runtime/continuation-store.js";
    29	import { ContinuationManager } from "../../src/runtime-state/runtime/continuation-manager.js";
    30	import type { AlixConfig } from "../../src/operations/config/schema.js";
    31	
    32	const EFFECT_CMD = "printf 'effect\\n' >> effects.txt";
    33	
    34	let seq = 0;
    35	
    36	interface Fixture {
    37	  root: string;
    38	  log: EventLog;
    39	  store: ApprovalStore;
    40	  config: AlixConfig;
    41	  executor: ToolExecutor;
    42	  cleanup: () => void;
    43	}
    44	
    45	async function makeFixture(label: string): Promise<Fixture> {
    46	  const root = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), `alix-crash-${label}-`));
    47	  mkdirSync(join(root, ".alix", "approvals"), { recursive: true });
    48	  const log = new EventLog(join(root, ".alix", "sessions", "test"));
    49	  await log.init();
    50	  const store = new ApprovalStore(root, { eventLog: log });
    51	  await store.load();
    52	
    53	  const config = structuredClone(DEFAULT_CONFIG);
    54	  config.permissions.sessionMode = "ask";
    55	  config.permissions.tools = { "shell.run": "ask", "file.read": "ask", "file.create": "ask" };
    56	
    57	  const executor = createToolExecutor({ config, log, root, approvalStore: store });
    58	  return { root, log, store, config, executor, cleanup: () => rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true }) };
    59	}

    61	function effectCount(root: string): number {
    62	  if (!existsSync(join(root, "effects.txt"))) return 0;
    63	  return readFileSync(join(root, "effects.txt"), "utf8").trim().split("\n").filter(Boolean).length;
    64	}

    66	async function requestAndApprove(
    67	  f: Fixture,
    68	  overrides: { name?: string; args?: Record<string, unknown>; agentId?: string } = {},
    69	) {
    70	  const request = {
    71	    toolCallId: overrides.name === "file.read" ? `read-${seq++}` : `call-${seq++}`,
    72	    name: overrides.name ?? "shell.run",

    76	  const first = await f.executor.execute(request);

    79	  assert.ok(record);
    80	  await f.store.resolve(record.id, "approved", "crash-recovery fixture");

    82	}
    83	
    84	describe("approval crash-after-consume recovery", () => {
    85	  it("marks a claimed-but-unreported approval unverified and denies the replay", async () => {
    86	    const f = await makeFixture("unverified");
    87	    try {
    88	      const { request, record } = await requestAndApprove(f);
    89	
    90	      // Simulate a crash: the claim committed, execution never reported back.
    91	      const claim = await f.store.consumeApproved(record.id, record.bindingKey, {});
    92	      assert.equal(claim.consumed, true);
    93	      assert.equal(f.store.get(record.id)?.executionOutcome, "pending");
    94	
    95	      const replay = await f.executor.execute(request);
    96	      assert.equal(replay.kind, "denied");
    97	      assert.match((replay as { reason: string }).reason, /UNVERIFIED|unknown/i);
    98	      assert.equal(effectCount(f.root), 0, "an unverified claim must never execute");
    99	
   100	      const after = f.store.get(record.id);
   101	      assert.equal(after?.status, "consumed");
   102	      assert.equal(after?.executionOutcome, "unverified", "replay must classify the dead claim");
   103	
   104	      // A second replay is stable and equally denied.
   105	      const again = await f.executor.execute(request);
   106	      assert.equal(again.kind, "denied");
   107	      assert.equal(effectCount(f.root), 0);
   108	    } finally {
   109	      f.cleanup();
   110	    }
   111	  });
   112	
   113	  it("reports a proven success and still refuses a replay", async () => {
   114	    const f = await makeFixture("success");
   115	    try {
   116	      const { request, record } = await requestAndApprove(f);
   117	      const run = await f.executor.execute(request);
   118	      assert.equal(run.kind, "success");
   119	      assert.equal(effectCount(f.root), 1);
   120	      assert.equal(f.store.get(record.id)?.executionOutcome, "succeeded");
   121	
   122	      const replay = await f.executor.execute(request);
   123	      assert.equal(replay.kind, "denied");
   124	      assert.match((replay as { reason: string }).reason, /already executed successfully/i);
   125	      assert.equal(effectCount(f.root), 1);
   126	    } finally {
   127	      f.cleanup();
   128	    }
   129	  });
   130	
   131	  it("reports a proven failure and still refuses a replay", async () => {
   132	    const f = await makeFixture("failure");
   133	    try {
   134	      const { request, record } = await requestAndApprove(f, {
   135	        name: "file.read",
   136	        args: { path: "does-not-exist.txt" },
   137	      });
   138	      const run = await f.executor.execute(request);
   139	      assert.equal(run.kind, "error");
   140	      assert.equal(f.store.get(record.id)?.executionOutcome, "failed");
   141	
   142	      const replay = await f.executor.execute(request);
   143	      assert.equal(replay.kind, "denied");
   144	      assert.match((replay as { reason: string }).reason, /execution already failed/i);
   145	    } finally {
   146	      f.cleanup();
   147	    }
   148	  });
   149	
   150	  it("lists uncertain consumptions without a wall-clock sweep", async () => {
   151	    const f = await makeFixture("list");
   152	    try {
   153	      const { record } = await requestAndApprove(f);
   154	      await f.store.consumeApproved(record.id, record.bindingKey, {});
   155	
   156	      const pendingList = f.store.listUncertainConsumptions();
   157	      assert.equal(pendingList.length, 1);
   158	      assert.equal(pendingList[0].id, record.id);
   159	      assert.equal(pendingList[0].executionOutcome, "pending");
   160	
   161	      assert.equal(await f.store.markExecutionUnverified(record.id), "unverified");
   162	      const after = f.store.listUncertainConsumptions();
   163	      assert.equal(after.length, 1);
   164	      assert.equal(after[0].executionOutcome, "unverified");
   165	
   166	      // Idempotent: a second classification does not mint a new state.
   167	      assert.equal(await f.store.markExecutionUnverified(record.id), "unverified");
   168	
   169	      // A resolved claim is no longer uncertain.
   170	      const other = await requestAndApprove(f);
   171	      assert.equal((await f.executor.execute(other.request)).kind, "success");
   172	      assert.equal(
   173	        f.store.listUncertainConsumptions().some(r => r.id === other.record.id),
   174	        false,
   175	      );
   176	    } finally {
   177	      f.cleanup();
   178	    }
   179	  });
   180	
   181	  it("does not let a duplicate outcome report rewrite a resolved claim", async () => {
   182	    const f = await makeFixture("dup");
   183	    try {
   184	      const { request, record } = await requestAndApprove(f);
   185	      assert.equal((await f.executor.execute(request)).kind, "success");
   186	      assert.equal(f.store.get(record.id)?.executionOutcome, "succeeded");
   187	
   188	      const overwrite = await f.store.recordExecutionOutcome(record.id, "failed");
   189	      assert.equal(overwrite, false, "first terminal transition wins");
   190	      assert.equal(f.store.get(record.id)?.executionOutcome, "succeeded");
   191	    } finally {
   192	      f.cleanup();
   193	    }
   194	  });
   195	
   196	  it("classifies a crashed claim at the continuation seam too", async () => {
   197	    const f = await makeFixture("cont-crash");
   198	    try {
   199	      const { record } = await requestAndApprove(f);
   200	      await f.store.consumeApproved(record.id, record.bindingKey, {});
   201	
   202	      const cs = new ContinuationStore(f.root);
   203	      await cs.load();
   204	      const manager = new ContinuationManager({
   205	        approvalStore: f.store,
   206	        continuationStore: cs,
   207	        eventLog: f.log,
   208	        executeTool: (call) => f.executor.execute(call as never),
   209	      });
   210	
   211	      const result = await manager.resumeApproved(record.id);
   212	      assert.equal(result.resumed, false);
   213	      assert.match(result.error ?? "", /UNVERIFIED/i);
   214	      assert.equal(effectCount(f.root), 0);
   215	      assert.equal(f.store.get(record.id)?.executionOutcome, "unverified");
   216	    } finally {
   217	      f.cleanup();
   218	    }
   219	  });
   220	});
    80	    args: overrides.args ?? { command: overrides.command ?? EFFECT_CMD },
    81	    agentId: overrides.agentId ?? "agent-one",
    82	  };

    84	
    85	/** Run the call once (gated → ask), approve the resulting record, return its id. */
    86	async function askAndApprove(f: Fixture, request: ReturnType<typeof shellRequest>): Promise<string> {
    87	  const first = await f.executor.execute(request);
    88	  assert.equal(first.kind, "denied", "first attempt must be gated");
    89	  const approvalId = (first as { approvalId?: string }).approvalId;
    90	  assert.ok(approvalId, "gated call must carry an approvalId");
    91	  await f.store.resolve(approvalId, "approved", "single-use fixture");
    92	  return approvalId;
    93	}
    94	
    95	async function makeContinuationManager(f: Fixture): Promise<ContinuationManager> {
    96	  const cs = new ContinuationStore(f.root);
    97	  await cs.load();
    98	  return new ContinuationManager({
    99	    approvalStore: f.store,
   100	    continuationStore: cs,
   101	    eventLog: f.log,
   102	    executeTool: (call) => f.executor.execute(call as never),
   103	  });
   104	}
   105	
   106	async function persistContinuation(
   107	  f: Fixture,
   108	  approvalId: string,
   109	  request: ReturnType<typeof shellRequest>,
   110	): Promise<void> {
   111	  const cs = new ContinuationStore(f.root);
   112	  await cs.load();
   113	  await cs.persist({
   114	    approvalId,
   115	    kind: "tool",
   116	    sessionId: "test",
   117	    cwd: f.root,
   118	    toolCall: {
   119	      toolCallId: request.toolCallId,
   120	      name: request.name,
   121	      capability: "shell.run",
   122	      args: request.args,
   123	      argsHash: hashArgs(request.args),
   124	      agentId: request.agentId,
   125	    },
   126	    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
   127	  });
   128	}

    89	    try {
    90	      const raw = readFileSync(this.storagePath, "utf-8");
    91	      const store = JSON.parse(raw) as OwnershipStore;
    92	      this.records = (store.records ?? []).map(r => ({
    93	        ...r,

    95	      }));
    96	      this.revision = store.revision ?? 0;
    97	    } catch {
    98	      this.records = [];
    99	      this.revision = 0;
   100	    }

   103	  private applyExpiration(): void {
   104	    const now = new Date().toISOString();
   105	    for (const r of this.records) {
   106	      if (r.status === "active" && this.isExpired(r)) {
   107	        r.status = "expired";
   108	        r.expiredAt = now;
   109	        this.changed = true;
   110	        this.queueEvent("ownership.expired", { recordId: r.id, agentId: r.agentId });

   354	  private pruneUnlocked(opts?: { olderThanDays?: number }): number {
   355	    const cutoff = Date.now() - ((opts?.olderThanDays ?? HISTORY_RETENTION_DAYS) * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
   356	    const before = this.records.length;
   357	    this.records = this.records.filter(r => {
   358	      if (r.status === "active" && !this.isExpired(r)) return true;
   359	      const t = r.expiredAt ?? r.revokedAt ?? r.releasedAt ?? r.expiresAt;
   360	      return new Date(t).getTime() >= cutoff;
   361	    });
   362	    const removed = before - this.records.length;
   363	    if (removed > 0) this.markChanged();
   364	    return removed;
   365	  }
   366	
   367	  private authorizeMutationUnlocked(opts: {
   368	    agentId: string;
   369	    targets: MutationTarget[];
   370	    autoAcquire: boolean;
   371	  }): MutationAuthorization {
   372	    const { agentId, targets, autoAcquire } = opts;
   373	
   374	    // Phase 1: Check all targets for conflicts with other agents
   375	    for (const t of targets) {
   376	      const c = this.findConflictsForPath(agentId, t.path, "exclusive-write");
   377	      if (c) {
   378	        this.queueEvent("ownership.denied", { agentId, reason: c.reason, conflicting: c.conflictingRecords });
   379	        return { allowed: false, reason: `Ownership conflict on ${t.path}: ${c.reason}` };
   380	      }
   381	    }
   382	
   383	    // Phase 2: Verify or acquire coverage
   384	    if (!autoAcquire) {
   385	      // Explicit ownership required — check every target has coverage
   386	      const uncovered = targets.filter(t => !this.hasCoverageForPath(agentId, t.path));
   387	      if (uncovered.length > 0) {
   388	        this.queueEvent("ownership.denied", { agentId, reason: "Explicit lease required", uncovered: uncovered.map(t => t.path) });
   389	        return {
   390	          allowed: false,
   391	          reason: `Explicit ownership lease required for: ${uncovered.map(t => t.path).join(", ")}. ` +
   392	            `Acquire a lease with the ownership CLI first.`,
   393	        };
   394	      }
   395	    } else {
   396	      // Auto-acquire mode: every target must be confident
   397	      const unconfident = targets.filter(t => !t.confident);
   398	      if (unconfident.length > 0) {
   399	        this.queueEvent("ownership.denied", { agentId, reason: "Unconfident targets", unconfident: unconfident.map(t => t.path) });
   400	        return {
   401	          allowed: false,
   402	          reason: `Unconfident targets cannot be auto-acquired: ${unconfident.map(t => t.path).join(", ")}`,
   403	        };
   404	      }
   405	      for (const t of targets) {
   406	        if (!this.hasCoverageForPath(agentId, t.path)) {
   407	          this.acquireUnlocked({
   408	            agentId,
   409	            scope: { kind: "path", root: t.path, recursive: false },
   410	            mode: "exclusive-write",
   411	            reason: "auto-acquired by gate",
   412	            ttlMs: 5 * 60 * 1000,
   413	          });
   414	        }
   415	      }
   416	    }
   417	
   418	    return { allowed: true };
   419	  }
